### UE economizou € 120 bilhões em importações de energia desde 2022

### NUMA vs SMP: The Memory Lie Multicore Systems Tell

![NUMA vs SMP: The Memory Lie Multicore Systems Tell](<https://media2.dev.to/dynamic/image/width=1200,height=627,fit=cover,gravity=auto,format=auto/https%3A%2F%2Fdev-to-uploads.s3.us-east-2.amazonaws.com%2Fuploads%2Farticles%2Fy9rh2l1vej50km314cv0.png>)

A server with two processors has two memory controllers, and a core on one chip can reach the other chip's memory only over an interconnect.

### Greta Thunberg é condenada a pagar multa após invasão de universidade na Dinamarca

### Do AI Models Admit When a Hint Changes Their Answer? I Tested It on 183 Med MCQs.

### Astrologia védica e ocidental: saiba o que muda na interpretação dos astros
